- **implement-sdd-spec**: Drives an accepted SDD spec through implementation to delivery. Use when the user asks to implement a spec, build a spec, start work on a spec, resume work on an in-progress spec, or close drift on a delivered spec that has pending amendments. Walks tasks in order, produces code, fills proof artifacts with real outputs, runs the validation block, and transitions status from accepted → in_progress → delivered. Refuses on draft, proposed, and superseded specs. ([liatrio-forge/emerald-grove-pet-clinic-ryan-flachman](https://github.com/liatrio-forge/emerald-grove-pet-clinic-ryan-flachman/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/implement-sdd-spec.md))
